richard@mars:~$ sudo btrfs qgroup show /media/usb |
  awk 'NR>2 {
    total++
    if ($NF=="<stale>") stale++
    if ($NF=="<under deletion>") deleting++
  }
  END {
    printf "total=%d stale=%d under_deletion=%d\n",
      total, stale, deleting
  }'
